Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

By the convergence analysis framework applied to structures structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 18/899 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
